`apps/member/src/lib/memberLocale.ts` is the app's **one** language declaration: `MEMBER_LOCALES` is handed to next-intl's routing middleware (`middleware.ts` keeps no list of its own), offered by the My Profile selector, and compared against by `preferredLocalePath()`. The locale of a page *is* its first URL segment — that is how next-intl resolves messages, how `<html lang>` is set, which Clerk localization the layout picks and what `apiClient` sends as `x-locale` — so applying a member's stored preference means being on that locale's URL, and `components/MemberLocalePreference.tsx` is the single place that does it: mounted once by the locale layout inside `AppProvider`, it `router.replace()`s onto the same page (query preserved) under the preferred locale. A page must never switch the locale for itself. The decision is the pure `preferredLocalePath()`, which answers "stay put" for a member with no preference (the app's own default still applies), for a locale this app cannot render — one dropped from the deployment, or one the API grew first — and for a member already on theirs, which is the case that would otherwise loop. It is skipped while a superadmin impersonates: the preference is the member's, and the impersonating staff user chose their own locale in the URL. Language labels are `profile.language_<tag>`, resolved **before** `t()` is called and falling back to the tag in upper case (`memberLocaleLabel()`), the same shape as the admin's `lib/localeLabels.ts`. `AppContext` carries `preferred_locale` on its member profile and exposes `updateMember` so the selector can refresh that cache after saving — a stale copy would have the redirect bounce the member back to the language they just left.

---

## Domain Modules

### Admin (`apps/admin`)

| Module | Backend router(s) | Frontend page | Notes |
|--------|-------------------|---------------|-------|
| Members | `members.ts`, `member-centers.ts` | `[locale]/members/` (+ `/deleted`) | Canonical staff-level CRUD reference. **The list itself is the shared card list (#928)**: expandable cards whose header band and rows share one `LIST_COLUMNS`-derived grid inside one `overflow-x: auto` wrapper (#637), with the surface, band, cell insets and dividers from `listChrome.ts` and the five filters in the labelled `FilterBar` (#724) — the same shape Products and Training Plans wear, replacing the `DataTable` this page used to render. Presentation only: same columns, values, filters, actions and expand/collapse as before. Soft-delete + restore; `clerk_user_id`; `/:id/invite`, `/:id/reinvite`, `/:id/revoke-invite` for member-portal access. Removing a member with a pending invite revokes it in Clerk. **Expand is reading, `⋮ → Edit` is writing (#797)**: the expanded row opens with a read-only `PROFILE` section carrying the Member's complete persisted Profile — the same fields the Edit form submits, driven by the one `MEMBER_PROFILE_FIELDS` definition in `apps/admin/src/app/[locale]/members/memberProfile.ts` so the two cannot drift apart — and it holds no input, no toggle and no Edit affordance of its own. The Profile's own fields come from the list row the Edit form is seeded from (no second read of the Member); only the Member's centers are fetched, from `GET /members/:memberId/centers`, and re-fetched when a saved edit may have moved them. **That route is also where the sole-active-center fallback lives**: an empty `member_centers` list is returned as the gym's single active center (`soleActiveCenterId()`, `is_default: 1`, `assigned_at: null`) rather than as "no centers", the same rule `resolveMemberCenters()` applies on creation and `centerContext` applies to a member's own visibility — a gym with several active centers has no fallback, and there an empty list really is empty. The route checks the member belongs to the caller's gym first (404 otherwise), so an unknown or other-gym id cannot read back the caller's sole center. **MEMBERSHIP PLANS (#958, #1051)**: the section lives in the **Products & Services** tab since #1051 (it was Profile's under #961 §1) and renders the Assigned Plans page's own list, `components/assignedPlan/AssignedPlansTable.tsx`, instead of the metadata cards #958 drew — Plan, Start Date, End Date, Status and a `⋮`, with the chevron mounting the shared Assigned Plan card body `embedded` (Q3 `share`). Two tables, **Active** and **Past**, split on `is_live` (§4). The Member column of the gym-wide list is dropped and the Plan names the row, because a list already scoped to one person is not identified by their name — that is the whole of what `scope="member"` changes. The `⋮` is the caller's (`rowActions`): `Assign New Plan` · `Cancel Plan` · `Details`, gated on the row's own `is_live` so a historical row offers `Details` alone (Q2 `menu`). Nothing #958's cards showed is lost — the fee and the next billing date are the expanded body's PRICING section, Created by / Created at are in `⋮ → Details` — and the Status column reads `lifecycle_status`, the same date-aware projection the Assigned Plans page shows (`LIFECYCLE_STATUS_SQL`, now reused by the configuration read). `+ Add Membership Plan` is absent while the Member holds a live plan, because under #956 adding one replaces it and that operation is named on the row of the plan it replaces; the `ReplacePlanDialog` stays, since a Member covered by a family plan somebody else owns still holds one (#956 Q4) and the server still answers `409 active_plan_exists`. |
| Centers | `centers.ts` | `[locale]/centers/` | ORGANIZATION module, admin-only writes (`requireRole('admin')` on POST/PUT/DELETE). A gym's physical locations: name, code, address, phone, email, `status`, and an optional `theme_id` overriding the gym-level theme (a Center with none inherits it, shown as “<gym theme> (Inherited)”). Soft delete is refused (409) while any `member_centers`, `spaces`, `calendar_events` occurrence, `calendar_event_bookings` or `trainer_availability` row still points at the Center. **(#800) Expanding reads, `⋮ → Edit` writes**: the Edit Center modal is gone — `⋮ → Edit` expands the row into the inline form (Name, Email, Phone, Address, Status, Theme, then Cancel / Save Changes), and clicking the row itself expands the same card read-only. Only one of the two is open per row, and the header click is inert while the form is open. `+ Add Center` still POSTs a Center named “New Center” and now drops straight into that inline form. No API change: `PUT /centers/:id` receives the same payload the modal sent, built by `toCenterUpdatePayload()`. `apps/admin/src/app/[locale]/centers/centerProfile.ts` is the single declaration of the field set — the keys and their order, their read-only labels, their formatting (Status through the `status` namespace, Theme through the same inherited rule the Details modal uses) and the persisted-row → form-values mapping `startEdit` seeds with (`Center extends CenterProfile`). Two of its fields are `editable: false`: `code`, which the old modal already carried through its payload without rendering a control, and `created_by_name`. The Details modal and its `View Audit Log` button are unchanged. See “Read-Only Expanded Row, Editing Behind the Context Menu” in `docs/feature-patterns.md`. |
| Spaces | `spaces.ts` | `[locale]/spaces/` | Admin-only CRUD. Inline creation row (no pre-API-call); clicking row expands read-only accordion (General/Availability/Notes sections all auto-shown); Edit from context menu opens inline edit form; StatusBadge in row; Details modal with full audit info. Duplicate, soft-delete, availability hours, notes. **A Space carries no Activity Types (#801)**: the `ACTIVITIES` checkbox list, `GET`/`PUT /spaces/:id/activity-types` and the `space_activity_types` join table are gone (migration 197 drops it), and `POST /:id/duplicate` copies the Space's own fields only. Which Space an activity runs in is the Activity Type's own `default_space_id` — one direction, owned by `activity-types.ts` — so do not reintroduce a Space-side Activity relation. SELECT LEFT JOINs `gym_memberships` ×3 for `created_by_name`, `modified_by_name`, `deleted_by_name`. |
| Operating Hours & Holidays | `operating-hours.ts` | `[locale]/calendar/operating-hours/` | (#418) ORGANIZATION module, `calendar.operating_hours` feature flag. Two gym-wide (no center scoping) tables: `gym_operating_hours` (one row per weekly shift — `weekday` 0=Sun…6=Sat, `start_time`/`end_time`; split shifts are just two rows for the same weekday) and `gym_holiday_hours` (date-range exceptions: `is_closed=1` for a full closure, or `start_time`/`end_time` for special hours; `annual_renewal` flag matches the same month/day every year, resolved at read time rather than pre-expanded into rows). Holidays always take precedence over the weekly schedule for the dates they cover. `GET /operating-hours/weekly` (any ORGANIZATION-readable role) and `PUT /operating-hours/weekly` (admin-only, delete-all-then-reinsert bulk replace, same shape as `PUT /activity-types/:id/eligible-plans`) manage the weekly grid; `GET/POST/PUT/DELETE /operating-hours/holidays` is standard soft-delete CRUD. `GET /me/operating-hours` (member-role gated, not ORGANIZATION-gated — this data isn't sensitive) exposes the same weekly+holiday rows read-only for the member calendar. Pure resolution logic lives in `domain/operatingHours.ts` (`resolveEffectiveHours`, unit-tested, no DB) — used by neither router directly yet; the routers just serve raw rows, and both the Admin and Member calendars compute FullCalendar `businessHours` (from the weekly rows) plus grey `display:'background'` events for holiday exceptions client-side (`apps/admin/src/lib/operatingHoursDisplay.ts` and its member-app copy — expands `annual_renewal` holidays onto whichever years the visible calendar range touches). If a gym has configured no weekly hours at all, every date resolves as unrestricted (open) rather than closed, so existing gyms aren't suddenly greyed out the moment this ships. The Admin calendar renders the same grey shading for reference only — no `selectConstraint` — staff can freely create events/sessions outside these hours; the Member calendar has no drag-to-select booking flow today, so the grey-out there is purely visual (booking always targets an existing, staff-created class session). |
| Staff | `staff.ts`, `staff-centers.ts` | `[locale]/staff/` | ORGANIZATION module, `organization.staff` feature flag. HR/employment CRUD (`staff` table, separate from `gym_memberships`/auth) with soft-delete, deactivate, duplicate. (#440) Expanded row is a single consolidated scroll (no section tabs). Multi-center association mirrors Members' `member_centers` pattern: `staff_centers` join table (`gym_id`/`staff_id`/`center_id`, `is_default`, one-active-default-per-staff generated-column unique index) replaces the old single `assigned_center_id` column (migrations 136–137). `GET/PUT /staff/:staffId/centers` (admin-only write) mirrors `member-centers.ts`; `resolveStaffCenters()` in `staff.ts` mirrors `resolveMemberCenters()` for create/duplicate, except a staff member may have zero centers (no access-control path depends on staff having one, unlike members via `centerContext`). Centers UI (checkboxes + default `<select>`) hidden for single-center gyms, matching Members' `showCenters` pattern. **(#592) Owns the login**: `staff.gym_membership_id` links to the `gym_memberships` row; see "Staff app access" below. The row's App access section shows status/role and offers Send/Resend invitation and Revoke. **(#798) Expanding reads, `⋮ → Edit` writes**: the expanded row is the complete Staff record read-only — Personal / Contact / Employment / Schedule / Notes, then Assigned Centers + Default Center (from the same `GET /staff/:staffId/centers`, "No centers assigned" when there are none, never an assumed default) and App access state (status, derived role, Clerk user id). It holds no input, select, textarea, button or Edit affordance; the inline form, with the invite/resend/revoke actions still inside it, now opens only from the new `⋮ → Edit` item (disabled for read-only roles). No new endpoint and no second Staff representation: both halves render the list row, and `apps/admin/src/app/[locale]/staff/staffProfile.ts` is the single declaration of the field set — the keys and their order, their labels, their read-only formatting and the persisted-row → form-values mapping `startEdit` seeds with (`StaffMember extends StaffProfile`). See "Read-Only Expanded Row, Editing Behind the Context Menu" in `docs/feature-patterns.md`. |
| Trainers | `trainers.ts` | `[locale]/trainers/` | Lists the gym's assignable trainers with availability management. Speciality concept removed in #219. **Since #986** that list is every **active Staff record** (`staff.employment_status = 'active'`, any profile) rather than the coach-role `gym_memberships` rows it used to filter on, read through the one shared fragment in `domain/trainerAssignment.ts` and ordered by first then last name (§5). |
| Professional Services | `professional-services.ts` | `[locale]/professional-services/` | (#484) Stable catalog of services a gym offers (Personal Training Individual/Duo, Group Class, Nutrition Coaching, Physiotherapy seeded as system rows), deliberately independent from Activities/Products/Plans/Staff Roles — no FK to any of them. `professional_services` (migration 140): `gym_id` nullable — `NULL` + `is_system=1` for the 5 global system rows, a set value + `is_system=0` for a gym's own custom rows; `system_key` (nullable unique) identifies seeded rows without depending on the translatable `name`; soft-delete columns apply to custom rows only (system rows are 403'd at the API layer, never soft-deleted). `gym_professional_services` join table (`gym_id`, `professional_service_id`, `status` 'active'/'inactive', unique on the pair) holds the *per-gym* enable state so the same system service can be active in one gym and inactive in another without touching the global row — mirrors the `charge_types`/`products` global-catalog-plus-per-gym-config split. `GET /professional-services` inner-joins both tables scoped to `(gym_id IS NULL OR gym_id = current gym)`, returning each service with its gym-specific `status`; `POST /`/`PUT /:id` (custom only, 403 on `is_system`)/`DELETE /:id` (soft-delete, custom only, 403 on `is_system`) manage the definition; `POST /:id/activate`/`POST /:id/deactivate` toggle `gym_professional_services.status` for either a system or custom row, scoped to the current gym only; `POST /:id/duplicate` copies any visible service (system or custom) into a new custom, active, gym-owned row named `"<original> - Copy"`. New gyms (`POST /platform/gyms` and its `/duplicate`) are seeded with an active `gym_professional_services` row per system service, same as the `products`/`tax_rates` seeding already there. `recycle-bin` extended with a `professional_service` branch (custom rows only, `is_system=0` guard on both the list/detail queries and the recover UPDATE). Admin page: expandable-row/inline-CRUD pattern (no `CrudModal`) matching Taxes — inline `+ Add Professional Service` row, System badge, context menu (Duplicate/Deactivate·Activate/Edit\*/Delete\*/Details, \*hidden for system rows) — #802 fixes those three ends for this page alone: Duplicate first, the Deactivate·Activate slot second with **Deactivate** carrying `ContextMenu`'s shared `danger` style (Activate is not destructive, so it stays unstyled), and Details always last, which for a system row leaves exactly Duplicate/Deactivate/Details. **(#546) Linked from Session-type Products**: see the Products row below — `product_professional_services` is a many-to-many join owned by `products.ts`, not by this router; this router's own read/write/visibility rules (system vs. custom, per-gym `gym_professional_services.status`) are unchanged and reused as-is for validating which services a Product may link to. **(#647 stage 1) Linked to the calendar, and to the Member**: migration 168 adds a nullable `professional_service_id` (FK→`professional_services` `ON DELETE SET NULL`) to `activity_types` (index `(gym_id, professional_service_id)`) and to `calendar_events` (index `(gym_id, professional_service_id, starts_at)` — the exact lookup the availability projection will run). The Activity Type is the default: `scheduleEngine.materializeScheduleRule()` stamps its value on every generated occurrence, `classSessionsRouter.post` inherits it unless the field is sent, and it is a `PROPAGATABLE_FIELDS` entry in `activity-types.ts`, so retargeting an activity moves its not-yet-started occurrences through the existing `confirm_propagate` 409 flow. Both calendar routers accept the field on create/update (`PUT` never re-inherits — a hand-set occurrence keeps its override). `domain/professionalServices.ts` (`parseProfessionalServiceId`, `validateProfessionalServiceId`, `activityTypeProfessionalServiceId`) validates against services visible **and enabled** for the gym (`gym_professional_services.status = 'active'`) — stricter than `validateProfessionalServiceIds()` in `domain/productProfessionalServices.ts`, which by #546's design also accepts a service the gym has switched off. The Member side has no table of its own: `domain/memberProfessionalServices.ts` derives it from the session-type Products the Member holds, resolved through `product_professional_services`, over three sources — `user_class_packages` (live `sessions_remaining`, unexpired), `promotion_session` benefits of `status='applied'` promotions on `status='active'` assignments (read live, like `billing-simulation.ts`, since the migration-156 snapshot tables are still unwritten), and open `user_membership_services` attachments of `type='sessions'` items (`quantity × units`). `aggregateProfessionalServiceGrants()` is the pure fold: it de-duplicates on (kind, granting row, product) so JOIN fan-out can't double a balance, and sums the rest — the issue thread's "10-session package + 4 plan sessions = 14". Counts intentionally overlap when one package is linked to several services, since nothing records how a bundle splits. Served read-only by `GET /members/:memberId/professional-services` (`api/member-professional-services.ts`, MEMBERS module, `organization.professional_services` flag) → `[{professional_service_id, name, sessions, sources[]}]`. No UI and no booking behaviour change yet; #635 adds Plan-level Session Benefits as a fourth grant source. **(#647 stage 2) Weekly availability projection**: `GET /members/:memberId/personal-training-slots` (`api/member-personal-training-slots.ts`, same gating as stage 1) answers "which *recurring* slots could this Member take over the next 2 months?" for the Mon–Sun grid in the Member profile. No migration and no writes — it loads every non-deleted `calendar_events` row in the window whose `professional_service_id` the Member holds (stage 1's list, reused not recomputed) and whose Activity Type is bookable by them, then hands the rows to the pure `domain/personalTrainingSlots.ts`. A **slot** is the group of occurrences sharing the same *gym-local* weekday + start/end time + `activity_type_id` + `professional_service_id`; grouping in the gym's timezone (`gyms.timezone`, the same conversion `materializeScheduleRule` wrote them with) is what keeps a slot whole across a DST change. Each slot reports one `dates[]` entry per expected occurrence with a status — `available` / `no_occurrence` (closure, rule ended, occurrence deleted) / `not_scheduled` (cancelled or completed) / `full` / `already_booked` — plus `available_count`, `already_booked_count` and `fully_available`. A date the Member cannot take does **not** remove the slot from the grid, per the issue thread's Q5 answer ("if a slot is already booked or it is a festivity, system will silently ignore it"); `fully_available` still answers §1's stricter reading for a caller that wants it. #481 eligibility is asked through the new exported `isActivityTypeEligibleForMember()` in `api/activity-eligibility.ts`, which the booking access hook now also calls, so the projection and `bookMemberOnSession` cannot drift apart. The grid renders in the expanded Member row (`members/MemberPersonalTrainingSlots.tsx`). **(#647 stage 3) Recurring selection and Book**: `member_recurring_slots` (migration 169) stores the Member's *weekly pattern* rather than dates — one row per (`gym_id`, `member_id`, `iso_weekday`, `start_time`, `end_time`, `activity_type_id`, `professional_service_id`, nullable `center_id`) — because the occurrences the rolling window will book do not exist yet. The column is named `iso_weekday` (1=Mon … 7=Sun, luxon's numbering, the same base the stage-2 API speaks) precisely because every other weekday column in the schema (`gym_operating_hours`, `activity_type_schedule_rules`, `trainer_availability`, `training_plans`) counts 0=Sun … 6=Sat; the name is what stops stage 4's job joining the two bases silently. Uniqueness is a VIRTUAL generated `selection_key` (`CONCAT_WS` of the six identity columns with `COALESCE(center_id, 0)`) rather than a plain UNIQUE KEY, since MySQL lets NULLs repeat inside a unique index and a single-center gym would otherwise get no protection at all. `PUT /members/:memberId/personal-training-slots/selections` replaces the whole set (a full replace, so select and deselect are the same request; rows that survive keep their `id`/`created_at`) and rejects any identity the projection does not currently offer; `POST /members/:memberId/personal-training-slots/book` books the window for the stored set, optionally replacing it in the same call. Both are gated with `requireModuleWrite('MEMBERS')` on top of the router's module/feature middleware. Booking never reimplements a booking rule: `planSlotBookings()` (pure, in `domain/personalTrainingSlots.ts`) turns each selection into the `available` dates of its projected slot, and each one goes through `bookMemberOnSession` in its own transaction — capacity, waitlist mode, #481 eligibility, package-credit debiting and the `ceb_event_active_unique` duplicate guard all stay where they are, and a failure on one date cannot roll back the others. A date that filled between projection and INSERT would otherwise land on the waitlist, so a non-`booked` result is rolled back (by throwing out of the transaction) and reported as `full` — §3 says not to book a slot that has become unavailable, and staff can still waitlist deliberately through `POST /bookings`. The response reports `created`/`skipped`/`failed` plus a per-date reason (`already_booked`, `full`, `not_scheduled`, `no_occurrence`, or an error code). A stored selection the grid no longer offers (the Member lost the service, or the plan stopped being eligible) is kept, reported as `matched: false` and books nothing (§5), and deselecting never touches `calendar_event_bookings` — existing bookings stay under the normal cancellation rules. Admin UI: `members/MemberPersonalTrainingSlots.tsx`, now a checkbox per slot plus Save selection / Book and a per-date result list, with the write controls disabled for read-only roles via `useModuleAccess('MEMBERS')`. **(#647 stage 4) The rolling window**: `POST /recurring-bookings/run` (`api/recurring-bookings.ts`, `X-Internal-Secret`, migration 170) is the nightly job §4 asks for — an endpoint fired by `.github/workflows/recurring-booking-run.yml` at 03:00 UTC, following `POST /billing/run` rather than introducing an in-process scheduler (which would run once per API replica). It re-projects each member's next two months from *now* and calls the same `bookSelectedSlots()` the Book button runs, so the window advances simply because "now" moved, with no per-member bookkeeping to drift; dates it cannot take become `recurring_booking_skipped` member notifications, deduped on (slot, date). See the router's row in the endpoint table and `recurring_booking_run_log` under Database Conventions. |
| Activity types | `activity-types.ts`, `activity-type-schedule-rules.ts`, `activity-eligibility.ts` | `[locale]/activity-types/` | Admin-only CRUD (#70, redesigned #269). (#324) `is_shareable TINYINT DEFAULT 0` added — when true, this activity type's sessions can participate in shared-training bookings. `activity_types` table extended in #190 with `default_space_id`, `default_trainer_membership_id`, `color`; extended in #269 with `default_center_id FK→centers`, full audit columns (`created_by_membership_id`, `modified_at`, `modified_by_membership_id`, `deleted_at`, `deleted_by_membership_id`), and soft-delete. `gyms.timezone VARCHAR(64) DEFAULT 'Europe/Madrid'` added. New `activity_type_schedule_rules` table: one row per recurrence unit (type one_off/weekly/monthly; weekday 0=Sun…6=Sat; ordinal for monthly; start_time/end_time in HH:MM). Sub-router `/activity-types/:id/schedule-rules` (GET/POST/PUT/DELETE). `domain/scheduleEngine.ts`: Luxon-based occurrence generator + `materializeScheduleRule` (bulk-inserts calendar_events in chunks of 200) + `cancelFutureOccurrences` (preserves past, cancels future). **(#482, backend stage 1 of the agreed plan — see issue thread)** `materializeScheduleRule`'s `weekly` branch slices each occurrence's `start_time`–`end_time` window into `activity_types.duration_minutes`-sized `calendar_events` slots via the exported pure helper `computeSlotSegments(start, end, durationMinutes)`, instead of one event spanning the whole window — a trailing remainder that doesn't fill a whole slot is dropped rather than stretched; a window shorter than the duration produces zero slots. `one_off`/`monthly` rules and rows with a null `duration_minutes` (legacy data predating the mandatory column) keep the pre-#482 single-event-per-occurrence behavior. `POST`/`PUT /activity-types/:id/schedule-rules` responses now include a non-blocking `slot_warning: string | null`, set when a weekly window doesn't divide evenly by the duration or when the duration exceeds the window — surfaced by the Admin UI as an info toast on save (see stage 4 below). **(#482, backend stage 2 — preserving booked occurrences on edit/delete)** `PUT`/`DELETE /activity-types/:id/schedule-rules/:ruleId` no longer blindly cancel every future occurrence. `scheduleEngine.ts`'s `findBookedFutureOccurrences`/`partitionBookedFutureOccurrences` split a rule's booked (`calendar_event_bookings.status='booked'`) future occurrences into those that still fit a proposed `RuleWindowConfig` (preserved — passed to `cancelFutureOccurrences(ruleId, {preserveIds})`, so they're excluded from the cancel and never regenerated, keeping their `calendar_event_id` stable) and those that don't ("impacted"). On `PUT`, this check is skipped entirely (old unconditional cancel+regenerate behavior) when the submitted window is byte-for-byte identical to the stored one — that's the #366 member-reassignment path (staff swapping which Members are auto-booked on an unchanged schedule), a different, intentional action from narrowing/moving the availability window itself. When the window *did* change and any occurrence is impacted, the request is rejected with `409 {error: 'booked_occurrences_impacted', impacted_occurrences, member_count}` unless the body carries `confirm_cancel_booked: true` (DELETE takes it as the `?confirm_cancel_booked=true` query param instead, since deleting the rule removes the window entirely — every booked future occurrence counts as impacted). On confirm, the impacted occurrences are cancelled and every affected member gets an `event_cancelled` notification via `sendBulkNotification` (mirrors `calendar-events.ts`'s `classSessionsRouter.post('/:id/cancel')` pattern) — no email, since the codebase has no mailer/outbound-email infrastructure at all; only the existing in-app `member_notifications` channel is used. `materializeScheduleRule` now also de-duplicates: before inserting, it skips any generated slot whose `starts_at` already exists as a non-cancelled row for the rule, so a preserved booked occurrence is never doubled up by regeneration. **(#482, backend stage 3 — overlap warnings between rules)** `activity_type_schedule_rules` has no space/trainer of its own — both are inherited from the parent Activity Type's `default_space_id`/`default_trainer_membership_id`. `scheduleEngine.ts`'s `rulesOverlap(a, b)` is a pure weekday-set ∩ + date-range ∩ + time-of-day-interval ∩ comparator over two `RuleWindowConfig`s (a `monthly` rule is compared by its configured weekday only, ignoring `ordinal` — a deliberate over-approximation for a non-blocking warning, never an under-approximation); `findOverlappingRules(gymId, excludeRuleId, spaceId, trainerMembershipId)` fetches every other rule in the gym whose Activity Type resolves to the same space and/or trainer. `POST`/`PUT /activity-types/:id/schedule-rules` call both together (`computeOverlapWarning`) and surface a non-blocking `overlap_warning: string | null` alongside the existing `slot_warning` — same advisory shape, no confirm-to-proceed gate, since a shared trainer/space isn't necessarily wrong (e.g. two trainers legitimately running different Personal Training slots in the same room). **(#482, stage 4 — Admin UI, final stage)** No new Availability section/form was needed: the existing "Schedule" section's `weekly` rule form (multi-weekday select + start/end time, reused across arbitrary Activity Types) already generates duration-sliced bookable slots per stages 1–3 with no per-page changes. The Activity Types admin page (`[locale]/activity-types/page.tsx`) now surfaces the two advisory fields from `POST`/`PUT /schedule-rules` as info toasts after a rule is saved, and handles the `409 booked_occurrences_impacted` response from rule edit/delete with a `ConfirmDialog` (same shape as the existing row-delete confirm, and the same 409-then-confirm pattern as `NewTrainingPlanDialog`'s #67 keep-vs-expire flow) that resubmits the request with `confirm_cancel_booked: true` once staff confirms. `calendar_events.schedule_rule_id FK→activity_type_schedule_rules ON DELETE SET NULL`. `POST /:id/duplicate` deep-copies rules and materializes calendar events. `POST /:id/restore` restores soft-deleted types. `recycle-bin` extended with `activity_type` branch. Admin page: expandable-row UX — inline `+ Create Activity Type` row (Name/Duration/Capacity), collapsed header (Name/Description/Duration·Capacity/Default Center/Status/ContextMenu), expanded General + Schedule (inline rule CRUD) + Metadata sections; center→space linked dropdowns. **Eligible Membership Plans + public_event (migration 139, #481)**: `activity_types.public_event TINYINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1` — deliberately defaults to true (both the column default and the create-API default when the field is omitted) so every pre-existing/zero-config activity type stays bookable by anyone; staff must explicitly uncheck it to restrict. New join table `activity_type_eligible_plans` (gym_id, `activity_type_id FK→activity_types` CASCADE, `membership_plan_id FK→membership_plans` CASCADE, UNIQUE on the pair) — the inverse of `membership_plan_centers`'s semantics: there an empty table means unrestricted; here `public_event=true` means unrestricted regardless of the table's contents, and the table only gates bookings when `public_event=false`. `GET/PUT /activity-types/:id/eligible-plans` (PUT admin-only, replace-all, validates every id against an active plan of the gym, mirrors `spaces.ts`'s `/:id/activity-types` pattern). Enforced at booking time by `activity-eligibility.ts`'s `registerBookingAccessHook` (see Bookings row) — a separate, independent gate from `plan-allowances`/`package-credits` entitlement checks; passing eligibility doesn't guarantee an available credit/allowance, and vice versa. Admin page: new "Booking Access" section (Public Event checkbox + Eligible Membership Plans checkbox multi-select, Spaces-style, hidden when Public Event is checked) in the inline edit form, read-only expanded row, and Details modal. **Waitlist mode (migration 154, #503 stage 2)**: `activity_types.waitlist_mode VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL` (`chk_at_waitlist_mode`: `disabled`\|`open`\|`closed`), overridable per occurrence by the nullable `calendar_events.waitlist_mode` — unlike `public_event`, this defaults to the *restrictive* value (`disabled`) for anything created from here on, while the migration lands every pre-existing row on `open` to preserve the old unconditional-waitlist behavior. Accepted/validated by `POST`/`PUT /activity-types` and copied by `POST /:id/duplicate`; the selector lives in the same Booking & Access section of the admin page. Writing the per-occurrence override isn't exposed by the API yet (read and honored only) — see the Bookings row for enforcement. **Activity → future-event field propagation (#503 stage 3)**: `PUT /activity-types/:id` now propagates edits to `default_space_id`/`default_trainer_membership_id`/`default_center_id`/`color`/`max_capacity` — the same fields `scheduleEngine.materializeScheduleRule()` copies onto `calendar_events` (as `space_id`/`trainer_membership_id`/`center_id`/`color`/`capacity`) when an occurrence is first generated — onto every not-yet-started (`starts_at > UTC_TIMESTAMP()`), non-deleted `calendar_events` row for that activity type. Past occurrences are never touched, and existing bookings on affected future rows are left exactly as they are (only the listed columns are updated, nothing about `calendar_event_bookings` changes). Because this can silently move/reassign already-visible future events, the endpoint returns `409 {error: 'future_events_impacted', fields, impacted_events, booked_events}` (naming which fields changed and how many future events — and how many of those already have a `'booked'` `calendar_event_bookings` row — would be touched) unless the request body includes `confirm_propagate: true`; this mirrors the `409 booked_occurrences_impacted`/`confirm_cancel_booked` shape used by the schedule-rules PUT/DELETE above, but propagation never cancels anything, so there's no notification step. `waitlist_mode` is deliberately excluded from this set — it already reaches every future (and past) event live via the existing `COALESCE(ce.waitlist_mode, at.waitlist_mode)` fallback, so there's nothing to propagate. **Default Trainer validation (#986)**: `POST`/`PUT /activity-types` now parse and validate `default_trainer_membership_id` through `domain/trainerAssignment.ts` — it must be an active Staff record *of this gym* (the raw value was previously inserted unvalidated, so another gym's membership id satisfied the FK and crossed the tenant boundary), while a value identical to the stored one is treated as not a selection and passes unchecked, which is what keeps an assignment whose trainer has since left editable and preserved. Admin UI: `[locale]/activity-types/page.tsx`'s inline edit save handles the 409 the same way the schedule-rule edit/delete flows do — a `ConfirmDialog` naming the impacted fields/counts, resubmitting with `confirm_propagate: true` on confirm. **Activity end-date special case (#503 stage 4)**: `PUT /activity-types/:id/schedule-rules/:ruleId` now diffs surgically when *only* `end_date` changes on a recurring rule (weekly/monthly — `one_off` has no `end_date`), instead of the general #482 cancel-everything-non-preserved-and-regenerate path. `scheduleEngine.ts`'s `findFutureOccurrences`/`partitionOutOfRangeOccurrences` split *every* future occurrence of the rule — booked or not — into those that still fit the new `end_date` (left completely untouched, never cancelled or regenerated) and those the new range excludes. Extending the end date only inserts the additional occurrences `materializeScheduleRule` generates for the newly-in-range dates (its existing de-dup skips every date that still exists as a non-cancelled row). Shortening it cancels only the excluded occurrences via the new `cancelOccurrencesByIds`; if any of them are booked, the request 409s with the same `booked_occurrences_impacted` shape used elsewhere unless `confirm_cancel_booked: true` is sent, and affected members get the same `event_cancelled` notification via `sendBulkNotification` — no new schema, no new frontend code, since the Admin UI's existing generic `booked_occurrences_impacted` `ConfirmDialog` and the member app's existing `member_notifications`/`GET /me/notifications` UI (see the Members app row) already cover this exactly. Any other kind of rule edit (weekday/time/start_date changes) keeps the unchanged #482 behavior. |
| Calendar events + Class sessions | `calendar-events.ts` (exports both `calendarEventsRouter` and `classSessionsRouter`) | `[locale]/calendar/` | CALENDAR module (#614; TRAINING before). **#360 stage 3 (API consolidation)**: `class-sessions.ts` and `calendar-events.ts` merged into one file, both mounted at their existing paths (`/class-sessions`, `/calendar-events`) with unchanged request/response shapes — only the backing table changed. Both routers now read/write the single `calendar_events` table. **Through #360 stage 3, a `kind ENUM('session','event')` column set once at creation discriminated the two; #503 stage 1 (2026-09) dropped `kind` entirely** — every row is now equally bookable at the primitive level (see "Planned: CalendarEvent Unification" below). The two routers still partition the table, now by `activity_type_id`: `GET /class-sessions` filters `activity_type_id IS NOT NULL` (an occurrence of an activity type), `GET /calendar-events` filters `activity_type_id IS NULL` (a manually created entry) — this remains a mutually-exclusive, exhaustive split, so the admin Calendar page's dual-fetch (#326, unchanged) still renders each occurrence exactly once without duplicates. `calendar_events` absorbed the booking-relevant fields formerly on `class_sessions`: `capacity` (`max_capacity_override` in the `/class-sessions` request/response contract — kept stable for the admin schedule page), `allows_shared_booking`, `cancellation_reason`, `effective_trainer_membership_id`/`effective_trainer_confirmed_at`, plus a nullable `center_id` (required at the application level for a session-like row via the same `resolveCenterId()` class-sessions.ts always used; optional for plain events — see #479 below). `class_sessions`/`bookings`/`shared_training_requests` are frozen — no application code reads or writes them anymore (dropped in a later cleanup stage) — see "CalendarEvent Unification" below. Sessions: create/update/cancel by admin/coach/staff. (#324) `allows_shared_booking` — when true, a second group may book via a shared-training request; `PUT /:id` accepts it. **Shared training capacity (#323)**: `spaces.max_concurrent_groups` and `gym_memberships.max_concurrent_groups` (TINYINT UNSIGNED DEFAULT 1). POST validates concurrent-group capacity when trainer+space are both set — atomically locks concurrent sessions (`activity_type_id IS NOT NULL`) via `SELECT FOR UPDATE`, rejects with 409 codes `slot_not_shareable` / `activity_not_shareable` / `slot_fully_occupied` / `sharing_not_authorized` (last includes `host_session_id`). PUT revalidates on slot changes. `PUT /:id/sharing-authorized {authorized: boolean}` (requireModuleWrite TRAINING) — disabling returns 409 if concurrent sessions exist. GET SELECT includes `is_shareable`, `allows_shared_booking`, `concurrent_groups_count`, `effective_max_groups` (LEAST of trainer+space limits), `booked_count`/`attendance_present`/`attendance_absent`/`attendance_pending` (subqueries against `calendar_event_bookings`). `effective_trainer_membership_id` records who actually delivered the session (`PUT /:id/effective-trainer`). `POST /:id/bulk-present` marks all pending confirmed bookings present. `POST /:id/complete` — hard-blocked unless all confirmed bookings have non-pending attendance and a trainer is set. Events: manually created entries (`activity_type_id IS NULL`), full CRUD with space/trainer conflict detection (409, `checkConflict()` still scans all of `calendar_events` regardless of `activity_type_id` so a plain event can't double-book a slot a class session already holds). **(#479)** `POST`/`PUT /calendar-events` accept `center_id` (independent of `trainer_membership_id`/`space_id`; unlike sessions, no default is auto-assigned and no conflict rule applies to it — a plain event's `center_id` stays optional). A local `isValidCenterId()` check (`calendar-events.ts`) — not `resolveCenterId()`/`centerContext`, since `calendarEventsRouter` isn't mounted with the `centerContext` middleware — rejects a `center_id` that doesn't belong to the gym with 400. `EVENT_SELECT` joins `centers` for `center_name`. Admin `EventDetailsPanel` shows a Center `<select>` next to Trainer/Space, fed by a separate non-fatal `GET /centers` fetch (kept out of the page's main `Promise.all` since `organization.centers` is a feature flag some gyms don't have enabled). FullCalendar v6 day/week/month views, drag-and-drop and resize. **(#981)** The event box names the trainer and the space in the Day view (a line each, with the occupancy counts on their own line after them) and as one truncated `Trainer · Space` line in the Week view, composed by `apps/admin/src/lib/calendarEventMeta.ts` from the occurrence's own columns — the month cell is unchanged. Soft-delete, full audit fields, status CHECK. Clicking a class session on the admin Calendar page opens `ClassSessionDetailPanel` (enrolled member list with Remove/Add/waiting-list, Change Time, Cancel Event) instead of `EventDetailsPanel`. `GET /class-sessions` accepts `activity_type_id`, `space_id`, `trainer_membership_id`, `center_id` filter params. **(#930)** `GET /calendar-events` accepts `center_id` too, and the admin Calendar page's own **Center filter** (`[locale]/calendar/CalendarCenterFilter.tsx` — the control #930 moved out of `TopHeader`) passes it to both routers, so choosing a center narrows the calendar. A manual event whose `center_id IS NULL` is gym-wide and stays visible under every center (mirroring #478 on the member side); a session's center is mandatory, so `/class-sessions` filters strictly. Neither route reads `x-center-id` for a read — see "Backoffice: CenterContext". Note: `calendar_event_series` (#191's now-removed recurrence-template table/`recurrenceEngine.ts`) is unused dead schema, dropped in the CalendarEvent Unification cleanup stage rather than here — recurrence today goes exclusively through `activity_type_schedule_rules`. |
| Bookings | `bookings.ts` | (inside Calendar page's `ClassSessionDetailPanel`) | Waitlist, capacity, attendance — backed by `calendar_event_bookings` (**#360 stage 3**; was `bookings`, scoped to `class_sessions`). `attendance_status VARCHAR(20)` ('pending'\|'present'\|'absent') tracks attendance separately from the booking lifecycle `status` ('booked'\|'waitlisted'\|'cancelled'). `class_session_id` is kept as the request/response field name everywhere (`/bookings`, `/me/bookings`, `/me/shared-training-requests`, `ClassSessionDetailPanel`, the member apps) — internally aliased from `calendar_event_bookings.calendar_event_id` — so no consumer needed to change. `POST /:id/attendance` sets attendance_status (staff-level: admin/front_desk/trainer roles). `POST /bookings` accepts `force=true` to add a walk-in over capacity (always inserts as 'booked', returns `over_capacity: true`). Credit consume/refund hooks via `package-credits` and `plan-allowances` (both repointed at `calendar_event_bookings`/`calendar_events`), plus the `activity-eligibility` access hook (see Activity types row). **Staff override (#481)**: `AccessHook`'s optional 6th param `opts?: { overrideAccess?: boolean }` and `bookMemberOnSession`'s trailing `overrideAccess` param let staff bypass any access-hook rejection (plan-not-eligible, plan-required, allowance-exhausted, center-not-covered) the same way `force` already bypasses the capacity check — `POST /bookings`'s `override_eligibility: true` body field threads through to every hook in the main booking loop (not the waitlist-promotion loop, which keeps its existing best-effort try/catch); `plan-allowances`' hook returns immediately when `opts?.overrideAccess` is set, and `activity-eligibility`'s hook does the same. The `POST /bookings` catch block now also returns `code: err.code` alongside `error: err.message` so the frontend can branch on the specific failure (`ClassSessionDetailPanel`'s over-capacity confirm-and-retry pattern is mirrored for these codes). **#372 cancellation-timing refund rule**: `cancelBooking` only auto-refunds a spent package credit when the member cancels at least 1 day before the session; a same-day cancellation keeps the credit consumed by default. `POST /:id/refund-credit` (requireRole admin/front_desk/trainer_performance/trainer_perf_nutrition) lets staff explicitly refund a same-day-cancelled booking's credit — only works while `user_class_package_id` is still set, so a second call 400s instead of double-refunding; `class_package_transactions` gained a `calendar_event_booking_id` FK (nullable, alongside the now-dead legacy `booking_id` FK → `bookings`, left as-is rather than repointed since there's no id correspondence between the old and new booking tables) for this. `class-sessions.ts`'s `POST /:id/walk-in` (same roles) books a member with no prior reservation and immediately marks them present in one transaction — reuses `bookMemberOnSession(force=true)`; rejects with 409 if the member already has an active booking for that session. **Waitlist mode (#503 stage 2, migration 154)**: waitlisting is no longer unconditional. `bookMemberOnSession` resolves `COALESCE(ce.waitlist_mode, at.waitlist_mode)` (same fallback shape as `COALESCE(ce.capacity, at.max_capacity)`) and only ever creates a `waitlisted` row while that effective mode is `'open'` — otherwise it throws 409 `session_full_waitlist_not_open` (over-capacity fallback) or `waitlist_not_open` (staff's explicit `waitlist: true`). `force` is unaffected: it books past capacity without touching the waitlist. `cancelBooking`'s auto-promotion is deliberately never gated on the mode — `'closed'` means "not accepting new members", not "drop the queue". See `docs/decisions.md` #12. |
| Shared training requests | `shared-training-requests.ts` | — (#437 removed the admin `[locale]/schedule/` page and its `SharedTrainingPanel`; no replacement admin UI exists yet for approving/rejecting requests) | (#324) TRAINING module + `calendar.member_calendar` flag. Backed by `calendar_event_shared_training_requests` (**#360 stage 3**; was `shared_training_requests`, FK renamed `class_session_id` → `calendar_event_id`, `ON DELETE CASCADE` from `calendar_events`). `class_session_id` kept as the request/response field name (aliased from `calendar_event_id`). Table: id, gym_id (FK), calendar_event_id (FK, CASCADE), requesting_member_id (FK members), activity_type_id (FK), status CHECK (pending/approved/rejected/cancelled), reviewed_by_membership_id (FK gym_memberships ON DELETE SET NULL), reviewed_at, created_at. UNIQUE (gym_id, calendar_event_id, requesting_member_id). `GET /?status&class_session_id` — trainers see only their sessions' requests. `POST /:id/approve` — transactional: locks request + session rows (FOR UPDATE), re-checks is_shareable + allows_shared_booking + `booked_count - effective_capacity < 1` (V1 max 2 groups), INSERTs a `calendar_event_bookings` row directly (bypasses access hooks — explicit override), fires `shared_training_approved` notification. `POST /:id/reject` — sets status='rejected', fires `shared_training_rejected` notification. Both write-actions use `requireModuleWrite('TRAINING')`. |
| Member Notifications | — (member-facing via `/me/*`) | `[locale]/notifications/` | (#194) `member_notifications` table: gym_id, member_id, type, entity_type, entity_id, payload (JSON), read_at, created_at. 7 active types: booking_confirmed, waitlist_joined, promoted_from_waitlist, booking_reminder_24h, booking_reminder_1h, shared_training_approved, shared_training_rejected. Written fire-and-forget by `sendNotification`/`sendBulkNotification` in `infra/notifications.ts` — never blocks the response. Session cancellation bulk-notifies all booked members. `cancelBooking` returns `promotedMemberId` so the caller can notify promoted members. **(#1072)** Every writer here also hands the notification to `deliverPushNotifications()` (`infra/push.ts`) after the insert, which sends it to the member's registered devices through FCM — still fire-and-forget, and inert on a deployment with no `FCM_SERVICE_ACCOUNTS`. See *Planned: Mobile app shell* below for the whole of that path. |
| Plans | `membership-plans.ts` | `[locale]/plans/` | Admin-only CRUD (#70). `lifecycle_status` (draft/active/paused/inactive) + `enrollment_status` (public/staff_only, migration 125, #370 amendment — `closed` removed, mirrors `products.enrollment_status`; new/duplicated/archived/deleted plans default to `staff_only`) + soft delete. Nested: prices (validity windows), `billing_policies` (the Billing frequency `recurring_billing_interval`/`_unit` + `auto_renew`; the Initial Billing, Initial Service and Recurring Service pairs were dropped by migration 189, #635 stage 13 — nothing billed off them, and Billing & Duration is now the Plan's only billing section), `plan_allowances` (unlimited or session_count per activity type with recurrence), `membership_plan_centers` (optional center restriction), `plan_charge_benefits` (per-plan benefit per gym charge: FK `product_id→products`, `action VARCHAR(30)` — migration 091 replaced old `charge_type_id→charge_types` FK; UNIQUE on `(membership_plan_id, product_id)`). `member_limit VARCHAR(10)` (migration 129, #374 — `'1'`/`'2'`/`'family'`, named CHECK, defaults `'1'`) caps how many Members a Membership on this plan can cover; validated on create/update, copied on duplicate; shrinking it is rejected (400) if an active Membership on the plan already covers more Members than the new cap. (#213) UX redesign: `+ Add Plan` creates an inline-editable row; context menu → Edit expands the row in edit mode with explicit Save/Cancel; Duplicate opens a new editable row. Expanded area shows Charge Benefits section (all gym charges with per-row action selector, replace-all PUT) and Period Benefits section (all gym activity_types with enabled checkbox + quantity + frequency). Row layout: Name, Description, Created By, Created At, Status badge, Enrollment badge, Actions (`⋮`). `GET /` and `GET /:id` include `charge_benefits` array in enriched response. New endpoints: `GET /:id/charge-benefits`, `PUT /:id/charge-benefits` (replace-all). Duplicate endpoint uses `db.transaction()`. (#375) Aligned with the Inline row CRUD shape (matching Products/Taxes): the page no longer imports `CrudModal` — `Details` from the context menu just expands the row read-only instead of opening a modal, and the former Add/Details/Price/Allowance/Centers `CrudModal`s were replaced by inline forms. Billing Policy, Centers, Included Services (allowances) and Price History each get a per-section `Edit`/`+ Add` toggle within the expanded row (`billingEditForPlanId`, `centersForPlanId`, `allowanceForPlanId`, `priceForPlanId` state), consistent with the existing inline Charge Benefits editor. New plans are created with a default `billing_policies` row (editable afterward via the inline Billing Policy editor) since the inline create row only captures Name/Description/Status/Enrollment, matching the ticket's mock. The "Members per Membership" select (Individual/Couple/Family, #374) is shown in the inline add/edit forms and the expanded Details view. (#409) `enrichPlan` also returns `products`: the full catalog of active (`status='active'`, not soft-deleted) gym charges for the gym, so the charge-benefits selector can be populated from the plan response instead of a separate `/products` round trip. Fixed `GET /:id/charge-benefits` to `LEFT JOIN charge_types` (was an inner join) — a benefit on a custom product (`charge_type_id IS NULL`, only system items have one) was being silently dropped from the response. **(#413) Applicable tax**: `membership_plans.tax_rate_id` (added by #311's migration 112/113, backfilled to each gym's system tax rate, but never surfaced until now) and the new `tax_behavior VARCHAR(20)` (migration 135, mirrors `products.tax_behavior`, default `inclusive`) are now wired up the same way Products already are: `enrichPlan` LEFT JOINs `tax_rates` and reuses `products.ts`'s exported `computePriceFields()` against the plan's `current_price` to return `tax_rate_name`/`tax_rate_percent`/`amount_excl_tax`/`amount_incl_tax`; `products.ts` also exports `validateTaxRateId()`, reused by `membership-plans.ts`'s POST/PUT. POST defaults `tax_rate_id` to the gym's system tax rate when omitted (mirroring the #311 backfill); duplicate copies both fields. Deliberately does not duplicate Plans' existing price (`membership_plan_prices`, a richer validity-windowed history) or renewal-frequency (`billing_policies.recurring_billing_interval`/`_unit`, already `day`/`week`/`month`/`year` with no `once` option) mechanisms with Products' flatter `amount`/`billing_frequency` shape — those were already returned by `enrichPlan` and shown in the Plans page's Billing/Prices sections before this ticket. Admin UI: Tax Rate + Tax Behavior selects on the inline create/edit forms (mirrors the Products page's `taxRateOptions()` pattern, `GET /taxes`); Prices section of the expanded row shows the resolved tax rate and excl/incl-tax breakdown of the current price. **(#547) Pricing section — one current price + immutable history**: a plan has exactly one price in force; `membership_plan_prices` is its history. Migration 161 adds `status` (`active` = in force, `applied` = in force and already pushed onto the plan's Assigned Plans, `inactive` = superseded; named CHECK `chk_membership_plan_prices_status`), `applied_at`, and a `tax_rate_id FK→tax_rates`/`tax_rate_percent` VAT snapshot per row (so a history row still reads correctly after the plan's tax rate changes or the rate itself is edited). `PUT /:id/pricing` (admin) is the Pricing section's Save: in one transaction it closes the current row (`valid_to` = yesterday, or its own `valid_from` when the row opened today) and marks it `inactive`, opens a new open-ended `active` row dated today carrying the selected VAT, and updates the plan's `tax_rate_id` with `tax_behavior` pinned to `'inclusive'` — the entered price is always the final VAT-inclusive customer price. A VAT change counts as a pricing change (new row); an identical price+VAT re-save writes no row. `POST /:id/pricing/apply-to-assigned-plans` (admin) copies the current price onto `user_memberships` on this plan whose status is `draft`/`awaiting_payment`/`active`/`paused`, sets the price row to `applied`, and returns `{ price, updated, kept_discounted }`: terminal (cancelled/expired) Assigned Plans and persisted `billing_events` are never touched, and a membership with a `discount_reason` keeps the fee that was negotiated for it (only `base_price`/`plan_price_id` are refreshed). Since #635 stage 15 the price it pushes is the assignment's own `membership_fee_price` — the number the resolver actually reads — and each affected assignment's snapshot is materialised first, because writing that column makes the row "captured". `recomputePriceStatuses()` re-derives `status` from the validity windows after every price write — including the legacy `POST`/`PUT`/`DELETE /:id/prices` window endpoints, which stay for API compatibility (the admin UI no longer calls them) — so the column can never drift from the dates it describes; a current row that was already applied keeps its marker. Because a same-day replacement leaves the superseded row with a same-day closed window, two rows can cover today: `effectivePrice()` (`user-memberships.ts`), `enrichPlan()`, and the admin Memberships page's mirror all tie-break `(status = 'inactive') ASC, valid_from DESC, id DESC`. Duplication copies each price with its VAT snapshot but never `applied_at`/`applied` (the copy has no Assigned Plans yet). Admin UI: the expanded row's "Add Price" button and price-window sub-form are replaced by a **Pricing** section (Price (VAT included) + Tax Rate + Save, live net/gross preview via `lib/priceVat.ts`) plus an **Apply new price to assigned plans** button behind a `ConfirmDialog`; **Price History** below it is read-only with an Active / Applied to assigned plans / Inactive badge per row. Tax Rate and Tax Behavior were dropped from the plan's inline edit form — price and VAT are edited together in Pricing, and new plans are always created `tax_behavior='inclusive'`. **(#485) Billing Events Forecast**: read-only, dynamically-computed projection of the next 10 billing events — never persisted (no new table). New pure module `domain/billingForecast.ts` (`computeBillingForecast`, `applyChargeBenefit`) reuses `billing.ts`'s exported `advanceBillingDate` for date math (same "4 weeks = 28 days" semantics as the real billing run, #369) rather than a second date-cadence implementation. `enrichPlan` calls it with the plan's current price, `billing_policies.recurring_billing_interval`/`_unit`, and its `plan_charge_benefits` (now also selecting `gc.amount AS product_amount`) and embeds the result as `billing_forecast: { available, reason, currency, events }` on every Plan returned by `GET /` and `GET /:id` — so the forecast recalculates automatically whenever price/billing-policy/charge-benefits are saved, with no separate fetch. Each event: `{ date, description, total, lines[] }`, one line per non-`no_benefit` charge benefit showing the gym charge's amount after applying its benefit action (`waive`/`percentage_discount`/`fixed_discount`, clamped at 0). Intentionally excludes Promotions (a separate mechanism, `membership-promotions.ts`) — the forecast represents the Plan's standalone billing configuration only. Also exposed as `GET /:id/billing-forecast` (returns just the `billing_forecast` object) for the ticket's explicit API surface; it's a thin wrapper over the same `enrichPlan` computation, not a separate code path. Unavailable (missing price or billing policy) returns `{ available: false, reason }` instead of guessing. Admin UI: new read-only "Billing Events Forecast" section at the bottom of the expanded row (below Price History) — no create/edit/delete controls, an explicit "does not include promotions" disclaimer, each event showing its date/description/total and line items with a `benefit — waive`/`benefit — 50% discount` style descriptor. **(#635 stage 1) Alignment with the Promotion benefit structure**: a Membership Plan now carries the same Billing & Duration and the same three Product-keyed benefit sections a Promotion has. Migration 173 adds `free_months`/`paid_months`/`bonus_months` to `membership_plans` (nullable, mirroring `promotions`' migration-102 columns, so "never configured" stays distinguishable from an explicit `0`; `pay_beforehand_months` is deliberately NOT added — it stays Promotion-only, as do Membership Fee Benefits) plus `membership_plan_session`/`_oneoff`/`_periodical`, the exact shape migration 155 gave Promotions (`gym_id`, `membership_plan_id FK→membership_plans CASCADE`, `product_id FK→products CASCADE`, `quantity`, unique on `(membership_plan_id, product_id)`, `CHECK (quantity > 0)`). `domain/productClassification.ts` gains `planBenefitTableForCategory()` alongside the existing `benefitTableForCategory()` — the same `classifyProduct()` decides the section for both entities, so an item can never land in a different section depending on what it is attached to. New sub-resources `GET`/`PUT /membership-plans/:id/{session,oneoff,periodical}-benefits` copy the Promotion endpoints' contract exactly (replace-all `{items:[{product_id,quantity}]}`; per item: gym-owned and non-deleted, positive integer quantity, no duplicate `product_id` in one request, and the category must match — with the same loosening that only a *newly* selected item must be `active`, so an already-attached item surviving a deactivation elsewhere is not silently dropped). `PUT /membership-plans/:id` accepts the three duration fields; each is written only when the key is present in the body (`IF(present, ?, current)` rather than `COALESCE`, which cannot express clearing one back to `NULL`), so a save from any other section never wipes them, and an emptied field restores "not configured" rather than writing `0`. `enrichPlan` serves `session_benefits`/`oneoff_benefits`/`periodical_benefits` with the plan, and `POST /:id/duplicate` copies both the months and the benefit rows. **Additive at the time — no billing change in stage 1**: Included Services (`plan_allowances`) and Charge Benefits (`plan_charge_benefits`) were left load-bearing until the Assigned Plan snapshot tables (stage 2) and the billing cutover (stage 3) existed. Admin UI: four new sections on the expanded Plan row — `BILLING & DURATION` (Free Period / Paid Duration / Bonus Duration) plus `ONE-OFF`, `SESSION` and `PERIOD BENEFITS` — each with its own Edit/Save/Cancel and no modal, matching #627's section-at-a-time editing. The two benefit renderers moved out of the Promotions page into the shared `apps/admin/src/components/ProductBenefits.tsx` (`ProductBenefitEditor`/`ProductBenefitView` plus the `benefitRowOptions`/`addBenefitRow`/`updateBenefitRow` helpers), presentational only — the owning page keeps the draft state, which is what lets the same pair serve both pages' editing models. Item pickers group by the server-computed `benefit_category`, never re-deriving the rule client-side. **(#635 stage 4, part 1) Charge Benefits retired**: migration 176 drops `plan_charge_benefits` and `user_membership_charge_benefits`; `GET`/`PUT /:id/charge-benefits` are removed, `enrichPlan` no longer selects or serves `charge_benefits` (nor copies them on `POST /:id/assign` or `/:id/duplicate`), and `computeBillingForecast` is called with `benefitLines: []`, so the #485 forecast is the plan fee and its cadence alone. The admin `CHARGE BENEFITS` section, its state/handlers and every `plans.cb_*` / `plans.forecast_benefit_*` locale key go with it; the Plan Details modal counts the three Product-keyed sections instead. **(#635 stage 4, part 2) Included Services retired**: migration 177 drops `plan_allowances`; the four `GET`/`POST`/`PUT`/`DELETE /:id/allowances` endpoints, the `allowances` field on `enrichPlan` and its copy on `/:id/duplicate` are gone, and the admin `INCLUDED SERVICES` section with its state, handlers and `plans.*` allowance keys goes with them. Nothing replaces it on the Plan: booking access is the Activity Type's own `activity_type_eligible_plans` list (#481), which the Activity Types page already edits — the relation the issue asked to invert. The per-window `session_count` cap has no successor. **(#817) Price History collapses; Current price shows gross + net**: the expanded card's PRICE HISTORY is a collapsible card that starts collapsed on every expand (`CollapsibleSectionHeader` — the header is a `<button>` with `aria-expanded`, the treatment #632 gave the Theme Colors groups; `priceHistoryOpen` is a per-plan id set that nothing seeds, and `toggleExpand` drops the id so re-expanding starts collapsed), and its rows are otherwise untouched. `Current price` renders `€60.00 VAT included (net €49.59 + tax = €60.00)` through `formatPlanCurrentPrice()` in `planProfile.ts`, which prints the server's `amount_incl_tax`/`amount_excl_tax` and computes nothing itself. **Which rate that split is taken at is decided once, server-side**, by `api/src/domain/planTaxRate.ts`'s `selectPlanTaxRates()`: a Plan's `tax_rate_id` is nullable and the Pricing editor offers that as **Default**, meaning the gym's system rate (`tax_rates.is_system = 1`, non-deleted — the row `seedSystemPtPackage()` picks), so `own` is what the Tax rate row displays (`null` ⇒ "Default", unchanged since #413) while `effective` is `own ?? system` and is what `computePriceFields()` splits at and `applied_tax_rate` reports. Before #817 a Plan on Default had no rate to split at, so the Current price read `—` for a Plan that has a price and the apply-to-assigned confirmation quoted an empty amount. One query serves both rates, so the list endpoint's per-plan round trips do not grow; a soft-deleted rate is still shown as a Plan's own (it is what is configured) but is never chosen as the gym default. No migration and no new endpoint. **(#820) Billing Frequency is a choice of two**: the Billing & Duration editor's numeric interval box and full `recurring_billing_unit` select are replaced by one dropdown — *Month* (`1 month`) or *4 Weeks* (`4 week`). No migration, no new column and no second cadence concept: the choice still writes the `billing_policies.recurring_billing_interval`/`_unit` pair that every assignment snapshots (migration 174) and `advanceBillingDate()` steps. `api/src/domain/planBillingFrequency.ts` (pure, unit-tested) is the single list of the two accepted pairs, and `PUT /:id/billing-policy` answers 400 for anything else — the rule lives on the route, not in the dropdown, so no caller can configure a cadence nobody sells. Rows written before the rule are deliberately untouched and still bill on their own cadence: `planBillingFrequencyOf()` returns `null` for such a pair, which is what lets the read-only row, the Details modal (`PLAN_BILLING_FREQUENCY_OPTIONS` in `plans/planProfile.ts` replaces its private `fmtBillingInterval`) and the editor keep showing "Every 2 months" rather than mislabelling it as Month — and the editor says out loud that saving the section moves the Plan to the selected frequency. Products' own `billing_frequency` is out of scope. **(#893) Mandatory Products are always part of the Plan**: a Product with `products.mandatory = 1` (#832) belongs to whichever of the three benefit sections `classifyProduct()` puts it in, whether or not the Plan has a row for it. The rule lives once, in `api/src/domain/mandatoryPlanBenefits.ts` (pure, unit-tested): candidates are the gym's **active, non-deleted** mandatory items only (an inactive or deleted one is ignored — the benefit `PUT` already refuses a newly selected inactive item, and forcing one in would fail every Plan save on an unrelated catalogue change); `mergeMandatoryBenefits()` is what a read reports, appending a missing mandatory item as `implicit: true` at `quantity: 1`; `withMandatoryBenefits()` is what the replace-all `PUT` writes, so a client that omits a mandatory item has it **preserved rather than rejected** — which is how an existing Plan picks one up the first time any of its sections is saved. A submitted mandatory item passes through untouched, quantity included: mandatory means the item must exist, never what its quantity is. Both `GET /:id/{session,oneoff,periodical}-benefits` and `enrichPlan`'s three embedded sections merge, and the benefit `SELECT` joins `gc.mandatory` as `product_mandatory`. No migration and no new endpoint. The enforcement is entirely server-side: the shared `apps/admin/src/components/ProductBenefits.tsx` takes an explicit `enforceMandatory` opt-in (the Plans page passes it to both halves of each section; Promotions pass nothing and are unchanged, #893 §9) and only renders a mandatory row as a labelled value with a `Mandatory` pill — no `✕`, and no picker that could swap the item away. Billing is untouched: a mandatory item reaches an assignment's Assigned Plan snapshot the ordinary way, as a stored Plan benefit row, and nothing back-fills Plans that were never saved again. **(#818) Example Timeline replaces the Billing Events Forecast**: the expanded card's last numbered section is now the Promotion card's simulation — a Period / Dates / Status / Billing table with **one row per billing period of the Plan's own cadence**, in the slot the #485 forecast held (`section_example_timeline` in `PLAN_SECTION_ORDER`). The forecast projected the next ten charges from price × cadence and never read Billing & Duration, so a Plan with a two-month Free Period forecast a charge for each of those months. New pure module `api/src/domain/planExampleTimeline.ts` (`computePlanExampleTimeline`) steps the rows with `advanceBillingDate()` over the stored `billing_policies` pair (a pre-#820 cadence included, stepped exactly as stored) and takes each row's Status from `classifyPlanDurationPeriod()` at its own start date — the same classifier the nightly run and `resolveMembershipFee()` price a cycle with, so the table cannot advertise a charge the run does not make. Until #892 the durations stayed calendar months there (#818's Q1 answer, "only the simulation"), so a 4-weekly Plan with Free Period = 2 showed *three* free rows; #892 makes the classifier count them in the Plan's own periods, in the run as well as in the preview, so it now shows exactly two. Row count is Free + Paid + Bonus + 2 trailing regular periods (Q5), capped at `MAX_TIMELINE_PERIODS = 60`, and the final row is open-ended (`endsOn: null`, rendered `N+`) only when it is a regular one. Each row carries `{ period, status, startsOn, endsOn, amount, waived }`: `amount` is the Plan's current price **VAT-inclusive** as `enrichPlan` already computed it (`amount_incl_tax`, falling back to the gross for a gym with no tax rate — the frontend does no tax arithmetic, #817), and `null` + `waived: true` for a Free / Bonus / Pre-paid period; a Plan with no price reads as the em dash rather than €0.00. `enrichPlan` embeds the result as `example_timeline` and `GET /:id/example-timeline` replaces `GET /:id/billing-forecast`; `domain/billingForecast.ts` (`computeBillingForecast`/`applyChargeBenefit`) and its unit test are deleted, since Charge Benefits — its only source of benefit lines — went with migration 176. Nothing is persisted, no migration, and no billing rule changed: Auto-renew and the assignment of a Plan to a Member were both declared out of scope on the thread. Admin: the table is the new shared `apps/admin/src/components/ExampleTimeline.tsx`, rendered by **both** the Plan card and the Promotion card (the ticket's "do not introduce a second simulation design") — presentational only, knowing nothing about plans, promotions, prices or endpoints, exactly as #806 keeps the shared Exercise editor free of them; the status labels, the Billing cell (`formatPlanTimelineBilling`) and the row tone (`planTimelineRowTone`) live in `plans/planProfile.ts`, and the Plan's statuses read *Free (benefit)* / *Prepaid (benefit)* / *Pay (benefit)* / *Bonus (benefit)* / *Pay (regular)* — the Plan's own vocabulary, never the Promotion's (#816 §14). **(#892) Billing & Duration is counted in Billing Frequency periods**: Free Period, Paid Duration, Pre-paid Duration and Bonus Duration are counts of the Plan's own cadence — "2" on a 4-weekly Plan is 2 × 4 weeks, not two calendar months. Migration 201 renames the four columns on `membership_plans` **and** on `user_memberships` (`free_periods` / `paid_periods` / `pay_beforehand_periods` / `bonus_periods`, with migration 189's CHECK dropped and re-added around the rename); no value is converted, because the numbers themselves do not change (§6/§7 — changing the frequency changes the unit, never the value), and a Plan on the Month cadence bills exactly as before. `api/src/domain/planDuration.ts` is still the single classifier: `PlanDuration` now carries the cadence it is counted in and `toPlanDuration()` takes it as a **required** argument, resolved from `ASSIGNMENT_CADENCE` for an assignment and from `billing_policies` for a Plan (`1 month` when there is none), so no pricing path — the nightly run, the Billing Simulation, `GET /me/membership`, the Payments dashboard's projected rows — can silently fall back to months. `FEE_ASSIGNMENT_COLUMNS` therefore selects the cadence pair (`duration_cadence_interval`/`_unit`) and `FEE_ASSIGNMENT_FROM` LEFT JOINs `billing_policies`. Admin: the editor suffixes each number box with the **selected** frequency (`× 4 Weeks`), and the summary, the Details modal and the Assigned Plan snapshot render through `formatPlanDurationPeriods()` in `plans/planProfile.ts` — `2 × 4 Weeks`, `2 month(s)` for a monthly Plan, `2 period(s)` for a Plan on a pre-#820 cadence or with no billing policy. **Promotions are out of scope**: `promotions.free_months`/`paid_months`/`bonus_months` and `promotionTimeline.ts` keep their calendar months, so a Plan period and a Promotion month can appear in the same simulation. **(#915) Billing Event Simulation**: a third read-only projection on the expanded card, declared last in `PLAN_SECTION_ORDER` as `section_billing_event_simulation` and answering a different question from #818's Example Timeline — one group per **billing date**, listing the Membership Fee and every Product the Plan carries (the three Benefit sections, mandatory items merged in per #893), each at the price its own `(action, value)` pair gives it (#896), with a per-date total. New pure module `api/src/domain/planBillingEventSimulation.ts` (`computePlanBillingEventSimulation`, `SIMULATED_CYCLES`) is deliberately a thin adapter over `domain/billingSimulation.ts` rather than a second engine (the ticket's "do not implement a second billing engine"): it expresses the Plan as the hypothetical assignment a member enrolling today would hold — no Promotions, no Additional Periodic Services, no Personal Membership Fee Benefit, and open-ended, since a Paid Duration says how long the contract runs and not when billing stops — calls `computeBillingSimulation()`, and does only two things itself: supplies the horizon floor and re-groups the engine's cadence sections by date. So every date, amount and Free/Pre-paid/Pay/Bonus decision is the engine's and, through it, `classifyPlanDurationPeriod()`'s. The horizon rule is the ticket's: a new optional `minimumCycles` on `BillingSimulationInput` raises the projection to at least `stream.start + N x cadence` for **every** recurring stream (bounded by the engine's own cap and each stream's end), so the length is derived from the frequencies present — a yearly item stretches it to two years and carries the 4-weekly ones out with it — and it only ever raises, so #629 section 6's first-regular-charge rule still runs a six-period Free Period through to its first paid period. Tax: the whole projection runs in VAT-inclusive euros, `enrichPlan` grossing up the Plan's price and each item's (`computePriceFields`, falling back to the stored amount when no rate is configured), which is exact because a Plan may only configure `no_benefit` / `waive` / `percentage_discount` (#896 section 16) and its durations only waive — so no tax arithmetic happens in the projection or the page (#817). `enrichPlan` embeds it as `billing_event_simulation` and `GET /:id/billing-event-simulation` serves the same computation (#818's pair, exactly); the benefit-section and catalogue `SELECT`s gained `amount`/`tax_behavior`/`tax_rate_percent` so no per-plan round trip was added, and `planCadence`/`exampleTimelineDuration` are resolved once so the two projections cannot step by different periods. Nothing is persisted, nothing is charged, no migration, and the Example Timeline is unchanged. Admin: `plans/PlanBillingEventSimulation.tsx` formats and never recomputes — the wire shape and the price-label mapping (`planSimulationPriceLabelKey`) live in `plans/planProfile.ts`. **(#916) One column grid for the three Benefit sections, with the Original and Final price.** The One-off / Session / Period sections were three tables sized by their own content, so `QUANTITY`, `FREQUENCY` and `BENEFIT` sat at a different horizontal position in each and none of them said what an item costs. The columns are declared once as `PRODUCT_BENEFIT_COLUMNS` in `apps/admin/src/components/ProductBenefits.tsx` (Product, Quantity, Frequency, Benefit, Original Price, Final Price — Benefit between Frequency and the prices), and `productBenefitColumns()` takes the flags from the page rather than the section, so every section of one card renders the same grid; the read-only view is a `table-layout: fixed` table with one `<colgroup>` inside an `overflow-x: auto` wrapper (#637's answer), and `showFrequency` is true for all three Plan sections — a One-off item's Frequency cell holds a `—` rather than disappearing and shifting the columns after it. The two prices are the server's: the new pure `api/src/domain/planBenefitPrices.ts` reports the item's gross unit price, that price after the row's own `(action, value)` treatment and the two line totals (`unit x quantity`) beside them, every figure from `applyLineBenefit()` (#896) — the same function the Billing Simulation's charge builders call, so a Benefit section and #915's Billing Event Simulation on the same card cannot quote one line two ways. The gross-up is `computePriceFields()`'s, extracted as `grossBenefitUnitPrice()` and shared with #915's loader, so no page does tax arithmetic (#817) and an item with no price reads the em dash rather than EUR 0.00. `enrichPlan` and all three section `GET`/`PUT`s report the pair, and an implicit Mandatory row (#893) is priced like a configured one — `loadMandatoryProducts()` carries the price columns for it. Nothing is persisted, nothing is charged, no migration. Promotions are out of scope: a Promotion's grants are priced against the assignment they are applied to (#919/#920). **(#946) The Pre-paid Duration is collected, not waived**: both projections on the card now show the Pre-paid Duration as the charge it is — the Example Timeline's first Pre-paid row carries `price × pre-paid periods` with `waived: false` and a `prepaidPeriods` count (its Billing cell reading `€210.00 VAT included · 3 periods prepaid`, and its row tone amber rather than the green "no charge" one, which is why `planTimelineRowTone()` now reads the row instead of its status alone), and the Billing Event Simulation bills one Membership Fee line of `quantity: 3 / regular_price: 210 / prepaid_periods: 3` in the first billing event with no further fee line until the fourth period. Neither page computes any of that: the rule is `prepaidPeriodsDueOn()` in `api/src/domain/planDuration.ts` and the amount is `resolveMembershipFee()`'s, so what the card advertises is what the nightly run charges. No migration, no new endpoint and no change to Products, benefits, waives, discounts or any other billing event. **(#962) The section reads *Membership Fee Simulation***: a label rename only — the heading and its two empty-state hints (`timeline_unavailable` / `timeline_empty`) say *Membership Fee Simulation* in en/es/ca, the wording the Assigned Plan card has used since #924 stage 3, and the key is `section_fee_simulation` in `plans`, `promotions` and `assigned_plans_page` alike (`plans.section_example_timeline` and `promotions.section_timeline` are retired). No endpoint, field, module, date or amount moved: `planExampleTimeline.ts`, `example_timeline`, `/example-timeline` and `components/ExampleTimeline.tsx` keep the projection's own names. |
| Benefit types | `benefit-types.ts` | (inside Plans) | Global lookup (no `gym_id`), seeded. |
| User memberships | `user-memberships.ts`, `membership-promotions.ts`, `user-membership-services.ts`, `billing-simulation.ts` | `[locale]/memberships/` | Status changes write a `status_changed` billing event in the same tx. Apply promotions to a membership. `GET /user-memberships` accepts optional `?status` and `?member_id` query filters. **`lifecycle_status` (#410)**: every row returned by `LIST_SELECT` also carries a read-only `lifecycle_status`, a date-aware SQL `CASE` projection of the stored `status` column for reporting/display — `pending` when `starts_at` is in the future, `expired` when an otherwise-active row's `ends_at` has passed, and `active`/`paused`/`cancelled`/an explicit `expired` pass through the stored `status` unchanged.
